Ruling: representation; i1040gi p. 42, Step 5, question 2: "Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee? Yes. Skip question 3 and Step 6; go to Worksheet B." The line validates but the reply does not (The reply is invalid: eic.h_qc_conditions, h_step34_ok, h_income_below_limit, h_earned_below_limit, h_wsb_below_limit, h_step5_ok and h_below_threshold put bare `all:` mappings inside `any:` lists without `op: test` ("malformed expression (project schema)"), and eic.h_qc_conditions also carries an empty `all`; triage executed nothing (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.deepseek.json`, state invalid)). By reading: the reply is a Schedule C (person.sc_count summed) or a person filing Schedule SE (person.files_se); person.files_se is person.se_line4c over zero, and person.se_line4c is zero without a Schedule C (forms/2025/person.yaml), so the second disjunct adds nothing and the line equals the project's eic.sc_count over zero. Not executed (observed equivalence not available). Project stands.

Ruling: model error; on validation and on the merits. The line reads eic.h_eligible, which depends on invalid helpers (The reply is invalid: eic.h_qc_conditions, h_step34_ok, h_income_below_limit, h_earned_below_limit, h_wsb_below_limit, h_step5_ok and h_below_threshold put bare `all:` mappings inside `any:` lists without `op: test` ("malformed expression (project schema)"), and eic.h_qc_conditions also carries an empty `all`; triage executed nothing (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.deepseek.json`, state invalid)). By reading: i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 2: "Do you, and your spouse if filing a joint return, have a social security number ... that allows you to work and is valid for EIC purposes ...? Yes. Continue No. STOP You can’t take the credit. Check the box on line 27c." i1040gi p. 41, Step 4, question 1: "Are you a married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household? No. Continue Yes. STOP"; question 2: "Were you, or your spouse if filing a joint return, at least age 25 but under age 65 at the end of 2025?" The reply's eligibility has no Step 1 question 2 test (household.all_eic_ssn, in the interface; stage13.eic_spouse_no_ssn, where the project gives $0.00 and checks 27c), no Step 4 age test (only an eic.died_2025 refusal; stage9.eic.no_child.over65, project $0.00), and its Step 4 question 1 stops every head of household by status alone, married or not (eic.h_mfs_or_hoh), as its Step 3 sends an unmarried head of household through the same-home questions asked only of a married filer. Project stands.

No. STOP You can’t take the credit." The line reads eic.credit, which depends on invalid helpers (The reply is invalid: eic.h_qc_conditions, h_step34_ok, h_income_below_limit, h_earned_below_limit, h_wsb_below_limit, h_step5_ok and h_below_threshold put bare `all:` mappings inside `any:` lists without `op: test` ("malformed expression (project schema)"), and eic.h_qc_conditions also carries an empty `all`; triage executed nothing (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.deepseek.json`, state invalid)). By reading, it checks the box whenever the credit is zero, including after the Step 1 question 1, Step 2 and Step 5 question 3 STOPs, which carry no 27c instruction (as the previous round, where triage found 204 such completed returns). Project stands.

Continue No. Skip questions 4 and 5; go to question 6"; question 6: "No. Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses" (amb.2025.separated_spouses_box). The line and its helpers validate but the reply does not (The reply is invalid: eic.h_qc_conditions, h_step34_ok, h_income_below_limit, h_earned_below_limit, h_wsb_below_limit, h_step5_ok and h_below_threshold put bare `all:` mappings inside `any:` lists without `op: test` ("malformed expression (project schema)"), and eic.h_qc_conditions also carries an empty `all`; triage executed nothing (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.deepseek.json`, state invalid)). By reading: it reads the filing status alone (eic.h_mfs_or_hoh), so an unmarried head of household with a qualifying child, who answers No to question 3 and has no spouse, gets the box if eic.same_home is answered no, and it skips Steps 1 and 2 (a filer above the Step 1 limit can get the box); its qualifying child count requires a valid SSN, which Step 3 question 1 does not. Project stands.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can’t take the credit." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A: "Use this worksheet only if you answered “No” to Step 5, question 2. Otherwise, use Worksheet B." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A, line 2. The line validates but the reply does not (The reply is invalid: eic.h_qc_conditions, h_step34_ok, h_income_below_limit, h_earned_below_limit, h_wsb_below_limit, h_step5_ok and h_below_threshold put bare `all:` mappings inside `any:` lists without `op: test` ("malformed expression (project schema)"), and eic.h_qc_conditions also carries an empty `all`; triage executed nothing (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.deepseek.json`, state invalid)). By reading: it looks line 1 up in the EIC Table on every return, with no shown_when and no guard for an amount above the table (a no-band lookup after a Step 1 STOP), and inherits line 1's Worksheet B error. Project stands.


Ruling: model error; on validation and on the merits. The line reads eic.h_below_threshold, which is invalid (The reply is invalid: eic.h_qc_conditions, h_step34_ok, h_income_below_limit, h_earned_below_limit, h_wsb_below_limit, h_step5_ok and h_below_threshold put bare `all:` mappings inside `any:` lists without `op: test` ("malformed expression (project schema)"), and eic.h_qc_conditions also carries an empty `all`; triage executed nothing (`reviews/2025/transcription/triage/eic.deepseek.json`, state invalid)). By reading: i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can’t take the credit." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A: "Use this worksheet only if you answered “No” to Step 5, question 2. Otherwise, use Worksheet B." The Worksheet A choice of line 2 or the smaller of lines 2 and 5 is right, but it is figured and shown after every STOP from a line 1 that ignores Worksheet B. Project stands.

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eic.credit | eic.died_2025 | - |
| eic.check_27c | eic.died_2025 | - |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    note: "For clergy and church employees the line uses an adjusted line 1z amount; no clergy/church-employee amount input is supplied, so this copies line 1z."
  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    note: "No Medicaid waiver payment amount or election is in the supplied input interface; treated as zero (no election to include)."
  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.step5_line1, take: eic.step5_line2}
  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    note: "No nontaxable combat pay amount or election is in the supplied input interface; treated as zero (no election to include)."
  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.step5_line3, eic.step5_line4]}
  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.earned_income, eic.h_eic_se_sum]}
    note: "Uses the catalog's per-person EIC self-employment earned income (person.eic_se) because the detailed Worksheet B Parts 1 through 3 inputs are not supplied."
  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: eic.wsb_4b, take: {const: 0}}
  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.earned_income}
  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs as defined earlier. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_table0
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_table1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_table2
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_table3
  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}
  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}
  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "If you have no qualifying children who have valid SSNs, is the amount on line 3 less than $10,620 ($17,730 if married filing jointly)? If you have 1 or more qualifying children who have valid SSNs, is the amount on line 3 less than $23,350 ($30,470 if married filing jointly)? If No, look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here. Look at the amounts on lines 5 and 2. Then, enter the smaller amount on line 6."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line3_table0
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line3_table1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line3_table2
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line3_table3
  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below_threshold, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below_threshold, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_smaller
  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_sc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_se, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_credit_if_eligible
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_eligible, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27c"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.credit, {const: 0}]}
  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_present, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_of_another_false, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfs_or_hoh, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_same_home_law, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Minister, member of a religious order who has not taken a vow of poverty, or Christian Science practitioner, with Schedule SE line 2 including an amount also reported on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [{const: false}, {const: true}]}
    note: "No clergy/church-employee status input is in the supplied interface; set false. The arithmetic covers the non-clergy case."
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero money helper"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: eic.h_qc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children with valid SSNs for the EIC"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn_child, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_qc_none
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "No qualifying children with valid SSNs"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: eic.h_qc_1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "One qualifying child with a valid SSN"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
  - id: eic.h_qc_2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Two qualifying children with valid SSNs"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
  - id: eic.h_qc_3plus
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Three or more qualifying children with valid SSNs"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
  - id: eic.h_qc_present
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "At least one qualifying child with a valid SSN"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: eic.h_mfj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is married filing jointly"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
  - id: eic.h_mfs_or_hoh
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is married filing separately or head of household"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
  - id: eic.h_qc_of_another_false
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Could not be a qualifying child of another person for 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
  - id: eic.h_same_home_law
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Either did not have the same principal residence for the last 6 months, or is legally separated and lived apart"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_qc_conditions
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 3 eligibility conditions for a filer with a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfs_or_hoh, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_of_another_false, {const: true}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfs_or_hoh, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_same_home_law, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_of_another_false, {const: true}]}
      all: []
  - id: eic.h_no_qc_conditions
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 eligibility conditions for a filer without a qualifying child"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfs_or_hoh, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_of_another_false, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [eic.died_2025]
  - id: eic.h_step34_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Steps 3 and 4 eligibility conditions"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_present, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_conditions, {const: true}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_present, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_no_qc_conditions, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_line7a_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040 or 1040-SR line 7a, or zero if a loss"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line7a, take: {const: 0}}
  - id: eic.h_investment
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Investment income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line2a, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, eic.h_line7a_pos]}
  - id: eic.h_investment_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Investment income is not more than $11,950"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [eic.h_investment, {param: eic.investment_limit}]}
  - id: eic.h_income_below_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 1040 or 1040-SR line 11b is below the Step 1 limit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_none, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit0.joint}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_none, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit0.other}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_1, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit1.joint}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_1, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit1.other}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_2, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit2.joint}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_2, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit2.other}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_3plus, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit3.joint}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_3plus, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.limit3.other}]}
  - id: eic.h_earned_below_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income is below the Step 5 limit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_none, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit0.joint}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_none, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit0.other}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_1, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit1.joint}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_1, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit1.other}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_2, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit2.joint}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_2, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit2.other}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_3plus, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit3.joint}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_3plus, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.earned_income, {param: eic.limit3.other}]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_below_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B total earned income is below the Step 5 limit"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_none, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_4b, {param: eic.limit0.joint}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_none, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_4b, {param: eic.limit0.other}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_1, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_4b, {param: eic.limit1.joint}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_1, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_4b, {param: eic.limit1.other}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_2, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_4b, {param: eic.limit2.joint}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_2, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_4b, {param: eic.limit2.other}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_3plus, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_4b, {param: eic.limit3.joint}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_3plus, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.wsb_4b, {param: eic.limit3.other}]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_positive
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B total earned income is more than zero"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.wsb_4b, {const: 0}]}
  - id: eic.h_sc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of Schedule C businesses"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.sc_count}
  - id: eic.h_has_sc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Has at least one Schedule C business"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_sc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: eic.h_se_files_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons whose files_se is true"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [person.files_se, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_has_se
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "At least one person has files_se true"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, gt: [eic.h_se_files_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
  - id: eic.h_step5_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5 eligibility conditions"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_positive, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_below_limit, {const: true}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_earned_below_limit, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Eligible for the earned income credit under the encoded STOP rules"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_income_below_limit, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_investment_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step34_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step5_ok, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_eic_se_sum
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total per-person EIC self-employment earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.eic_se}
  - id: eic.h_table0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, no qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_table1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, one qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_table2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, two qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_table3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, three or more qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line3_table0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table on line 3, no qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line3_table1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table on line 3, one qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line3_table2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table on line 3, two qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line3_table3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table on line 3, three or more qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_below_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 3 amount is below the applicable threshold"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_none, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {const: 1773000}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_none, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {const: 1062000}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_none, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {const: 3047000}]}
        - all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_none, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {const: 2335000}]}
  - id: eic.h_line5_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of lines 5 and 2"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb7_0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7, EIC Table, no qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb7_1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7, EIC Table, one qualifying child"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb7_2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7, EIC Table, two qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb7_3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7, EIC Table, three or more qualifying children"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 7 credit from the EIC Table"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb7_0
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb7_1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb7_2
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb7_3
  - id: eic.h_line9
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 9: lines 8 and 6 are the same"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.wsb_6]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 11 earned income credit"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line9, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb7
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line9, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below_threshold, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb7
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line9, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below_threshold, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_smaller
  - id: eic.h_wsb_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B line 11 smaller amount of lines 10 and 7"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.h_wsb7]}
  - id: eic.h_credit_if_eligible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Credit from Worksheet A or Worksheet B"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_credit
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.line6
```
